    \begin{align*}
        &e^{-\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{A_1\times \mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}}\frac{1}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
        &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{8C_5\left(3(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{8C_5(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv
    \end{align*}
    with some constant multiplication. To make the notation clean, we introduce a constant $C_T>0$ and write the goal by
    \begin{align}\label{eq:M[f]_integral_form2}
        \begin{split}
            &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{A_1\times \mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}}\frac{1}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
            &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv\\
            &\leq C(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}

    We start from the $v_z$-integral on $A_1$. We will show that
    \begin{align}\label{eq:v_z_int_res}
        \begin{split}
            &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{A_1} \frac{\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_z\\
            &\leq C\left(\frac{\tau}{t}\right)^{m_{2,2}}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    as the result of the integration. For this, we further divide the integral region into two parts:
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align*}
    and another part depending on the scaling of the exponential function in \eqref{eq:v_z_int_res}. If $\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}$, choose $N$ to be the smallest integer satisfying
    \begin{align*}
        N> \max\{m_{2,2}+2, -(m_1-n)\}.
    \end{align*}
    Using $x^N e^{-x^2}\leq C_N$ for a constant $C_N$, we have
    \begin{align}\label{eq:A1_bound_3}
        \begin{split}
            &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}\right)^{m_{2,2}}\int_{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)}^{\frac{z}{t}} \frac{|v_z|^{m_{2,2}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
            &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right) \mathbf{1}_{\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_z\\
            &\leq C_N\frac{t}{t-\tau}\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}\right)^{m_{2,2}}\int_{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)}^{\frac{z}{t}} \frac{|v_z|^{m_{2,2}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
            &\qquad \times \left(\frac{\sqrt{C_T}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|}\right)^N \mathbf{1}_{\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_z\\
            &\leq C\frac{t}{t-\tau}\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}\right)^{m_{2,2}}\left(\frac{t-\tau}{t}\right)^N\\
            &\qquad \times \int_{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)}^{\frac{z}{t}} (|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{N+m_1-n}{1+\beta}}\frac{1}{|v_z|^{N-m_{2,2}}}\mathbf{1}_{\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_z.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    As $N+m_1-n\geq 0$ and $v_z\geq -2(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        (|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{N+m_1-n}{1+\beta}}\leq \left(3(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)\right)^{\frac{N+m_1-n}{1+\beta}}.
    \end{align*}
    Also, since $\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > (|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}$ and $N-m_{2,2}\geq 2$, we have
    \begin{align}\label{eq:A1_bound_4}
        \begin{split}
            &\left(\frac{t-\tau}{t}\right)^N\int_{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)}^{\frac{z}{t}} \frac{1}{|v_z|^{N-m_{2,2}}}\mathbf{1}_{\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_z\\
            &\leq 2\left(\frac{t-\tau}{t}\right)^N\int_{\frac{1}{2}\frac{t-\tau}{t}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}^\infty\frac{1}{|v_z|^{N-m_{2,2}}}\,dv_z\\
            &\leq C\left(\frac{t-\tau}{t}\right)^{m_{2,2}+1}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_{2,2} + 1 - N}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    Applying \eqref{eq:A1_bound_3} and \eqref{eq:A1_bound_4}, we obtain
    \begin{align}\label{eq:A1_bound_5}
        \begin{split}
            &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{A_1} \frac{\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
            &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| > \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_z\\
            &\leq C\left(\frac{\tau}{t}\right)^{m_{2,2}}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n + 1}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
        
    Next, suppose $\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| \leq \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}$. In this case, we easily obtain
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)\leq |x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1\leq \frac{3}{2}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)
    \end{align*}
    Therefore, crudely bounding the exponential function by $1$, we obtain
    \begin{align}\label{eq:A1_bound_6}
        \begin{split}
            &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)}^{\frac{z}{t}} \frac{\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
            &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| \leq \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_z\\
            &\leq C\frac{t}{t-\tau}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1-n}{1+\beta}}\int_{-\infty}^\infty \left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}}\mathbf{1}_{\frac{t}{t-\tau}|v_z| \leq \frac{1}{2}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}} \,dv_z\\
            &\leq C\left(\frac{\tau}{t}\right)^{m_{2,2}}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    
    Combining \eqref{eq:A1_bound_5} and \eqref{eq:A1_bound_6}, we obtain \eqref{eq:v_z_int_res}.\\

    We move on to the $v_h$-integral. Inserting the $v_z$-integral result \eqref{eq:v_z_int_res} to \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_form2}, we need to obtain
    \begin{align}\label{eq:M[f]_integral_form3}
        \begin{split}
            &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}} \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_h\\
            &\leq C(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    We estimate this integral over the regions $B_1$ and $B_2$ defined in \eqref{eq:v_h_division}.\\

    \noindent\textbullet{} \textit{Region $B_1$.} For
    \begin{align*}
        |v_h|(t-\tau)\leq 2\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right),
    \end{align*}
    we further split the integral region by
    \begin{align*}
        |v_h|\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align*}
    and the other as in the $v_z$-integral case. If $|v_h|\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}$, we choose $N\in\mathbb{N}$ satisfying
    \begin{align*}
        N> \max\{-(m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1), m_{2,1} + n + 1\}.
    \end{align*}
    Using the inequality $x^N e^{-x^2}\leq C_N$ for some constant $C_N$, we bound the integral by
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq 2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\\
        &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_h\\
        &\leq C_N\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq 2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\\
        &\qquad \times \left(\frac{\sqrt{C_T}(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}{|v_h|}\right)^N\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_h\\
        &\leq C\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq 2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)} \left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{N+m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\frac{1}{|v_h|^{N-m_{2,1}}}\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_h.
    \end{align*}
    Since $N+m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1\geq 0$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{N+m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\leq \left(3\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)\right)^{\frac{N+m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align*}
    for $|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq 2\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)$. Also, as $N - m_{2,1}> n + 1$ and $0\leq (t-\tau)\leq \frac{1}{4}$, we have
    \begin{align}\label{eq:B1_bound_res1}
        \begin{split}
            &\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq 2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)} \left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{N+m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\frac{1}{|v_h|^{N-m_{2,1}}}\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_h\\
            &\leq \left(3\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)\right)^{\frac{N+m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} \frac{1}{|v_h|^{N-m_{2,1}}}\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_h\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{N+m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_{2,1} - N + n-1}{1+\beta}}\\
            &=C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2} + m_{2,1}}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    
    If $|v_h|\leq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}$, then
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)\leq |x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\leq \frac{3}{2}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right).
    \end{align*}
    Therefore, bounding the exponential function by $1$, we get
    \begin{align}\label{eq:B1_bound_res2}
        \begin{split}
            &\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq 2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\\
            &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|\leq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_h\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|\leq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}}\,dv_h\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2} + m_{2,1}}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    We merge the two results \eqref{eq:B1_bound_res1} and \eqref{eq:B1_bound_res2} and obtain
    \begin{align}\label{eq:B1_bound_res}
        \begin{split}
            &\int_{B_1} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2} - n + 1}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_h\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    It matches with \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_form3}.\\
    
    \noindent\textbullet{} \textit{Region $B_2$.} When
    \begin{align*}
        |v_h|(t-\tau) \geq 2\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right),
    \end{align*}
    by the triangle inequality, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{|v_h(t-\tau)|}{2}\leq |x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\leq 2|v_h(t-\tau)|.
    \end{align*}
    Consequently, we have
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau) \geq 2\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_h\\
        &\leq C\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau) \geq 2\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}(|v_h|(t-\tau))^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(2|v_h|(t-\tau))^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_h\\
        &= C(t-\tau)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau) \geq 2\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)} |v_h|^{m_{2,1} + \frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{C_T(2(t-\tau))^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_h.
    \end{align*}
    By taking $v_h\mapsto (t-\tau)^{1/\beta}v_h$ and using Lemma~\ref{lem:exp_integral},
    \begin{align}
        &(t-\tau)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\int_{|v_h|(t-\tau) \geq 2\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)} |v_h|^{m_{2,1} + \frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{C_T(2(t-\tau))^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_h\notag\\
        &=(t-\tau)^{\frac{m_1 + m_{2,1}+m_{2,2}}{\beta}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1} + \frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T}\right)\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|(t-\tau)^{\frac{1+\beta}{\beta}}\geq 2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)}\,dv_h\notag\\
        &\leq C'(t-\tau)^{-m_{2,1}-n+3}(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{m_{2,1} + \frac{m_1+m_{2,2} + (n-3)\beta}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{(2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1))^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T(t-\tau)^2}\right)\label{eq:B2_bound_2}
    \end{align}
    for some constant $C'$. If $-m_{2,1}-n+3\geq 0$, the desired estimate follows because
    \begin{align*}
        &(t-\tau)^{-m_{2,1}-n+3}(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{m_{2,1} + \frac{m_1+m_{2,2} + (n-3)\beta}{1+\beta}}\\
        &= (|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}\left(\frac{t-\tau}{(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\right)^{-m_{2,1}-n+3}\\
        &\leq (|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align*}
    for $0<t-\tau\leq \frac{1}{4}$. If not, let $\eta = m_{2,1} + n - 3$.
    Since $x^\eta e^{-x^2}\leq C_\eta$ for some constant $C_\eta$, applying
    \begin{align*}
        \exp\left(-\frac{(2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1))^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T(t-\tau)^2}\right)\leq C_\eta\left(\frac{(2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1))^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}\sqrt{C_T}(t-\tau)}\right)^{-\eta}
    \end{align*}
    to the final formula, we get
    \begin{align*}
        \eqref{eq:B2_bound_2}&\leq C(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}\left(\frac{t-\tau}{(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\right)^{-m_{2,1}-n+3}\left(\frac{2^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}\sqrt{C_T}(t-\tau)}{(2(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1))^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\right)^{m_{2,1}+n-3}\\
        &\leq C(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}.
    \end{align*}
    Therefore, we obtain
    \begin{align}\label{eq:B2_bound_res1}
        \begin{split}
            &\int_{B_2} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,2}-n+1}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_h\\
            &\leq C(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{m_1+m_{2,1} + m_{2,2}}{1+\beta}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    It also matches with \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_form3}. Combining \eqref{eq:B1_bound_res} and \eqref{eq:B2_bound_res1}, we conclude \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_form2} on the region $A_1\times\mathbb{R}^{n-1}$.\\
    
    \noindent\textbf{Step 2: Integral over $A_2\times\mathbb{R}^{n-1}$.} 
    
    We move on to the remaining region $A_2\times\mathbb{R}^{n-1}$, where $v_z\leq -2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)$. Here, for any fixed $m_{2,1},m_{2,2}\geq 0$ and $N\geq 0$, we bound the $v_z$ and $v_h$-integrals in steps by
    \begin{align}
        &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{-\infty}^{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)} \left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}} \frac{e^{-\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\notag\\
        &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + |\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_z\notag\\
        &\leq C\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N} e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{C_T}\right)\label{eq:A2_v_z_int},
        \intertext{and}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}|v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N} e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{C_T}\right)\,dv_h\notag\\
        &\leq C \left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\label{eq:A2_v_h_int}
    \end{align}
    for some constant $C$ depending on $\alpha$, $\beta$, $m_1$, $n$, $m_{2,1}$, $m_{2,2}$, $C_T$, and $N$. It shows that 
    \begin{align*}
        &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{A_2\times\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}} \frac{e^{-\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
        &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + |\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv\\
        &\leq C \left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}.
    \end{align*}
    By choosing $N\geq \max\{0, -\frac{m_1+m_2}{1+\beta}\}$, we verify that the integral on $A_2\times\mathbb{R}^{n-1}$ for the weight $|v|^{m_2}$ is very small compared to the integral on $A_1\times \mathbb{R}^{n-1}$.
    
    We start from the $v_z$-integral. Since $-v_z\geq 2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        |v_z|\leq (|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)\leq 2|v_z|.
    \end{align*}
    From this simple bound, we deduce
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{1}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\leq C\frac{1}{|v_z|^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}},
    \end{align*}
    and
    \begin{align*}
        \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + |\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)&\leq \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + |\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T|v_z|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right) \\
        &= \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + \frac{1}{2}|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T|v_z|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{t}{t-\tau}\right)^2\frac{|v_z|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T}\right).
    \end{align*}
    We use Young's inequality
    \begin{align*}
        |v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}\left|\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{\sqrt{2}} \frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z\right|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}\leq \frac{\beta}{1+\beta}\left(|v_h|^2 + \frac{1}{2}\left|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z\right|^2\right)
    \end{align*}
    to further bound the first exponential function as
    \begin{align*}
        \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + \frac{1}{2}|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T|v_z|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)&\leq \exp\left(-\frac{1+\beta}{\beta}\left(\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}\frac{|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}|\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T|v_z|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\\
        &\leq \exp\left(-\frac{1+\beta}{\beta^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{3}{1+\beta}}C_T}\right).
    \end{align*}
    Applying these inequalities and $(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)\geq 3(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)$ under this $v_z$ region, we bound the $v_z$-integral by
    \begin{align}
        &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{-\infty}^{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)} \left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_{2,2}} \frac{e^{-\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\notag\\
        &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + |\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\,dv_z\notag\\
        &\leq C\frac{t}{t-\tau}\left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}\right)^{m_{2,2}}e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{1+\beta}{\beta^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{3}{1+\beta}}C_T}\right)\notag\\
        &\qquad \times \int_{-\infty}^{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)} |v_z|^{m_{2,2}+\frac{m_1-n}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\frac{t}{t-\tau}\right)^2|v_z|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{3+\beta}{1+\beta}}C_T}\right)\,dv_z\label{eq:A2_v_z_int_1}.
    \end{align}
    For the final integral, using Lemma~\ref{lem:exp_integral}, we have
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{-\infty}^{-2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)} |v_z|^{m_{2,2}+\frac{m_1-n}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\frac{t}{t-\tau}\right)^2|v_z|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{3+\beta}{1+\beta}}C_T}\right)\,dv_z\\
        &\leq C\left(\frac{t-\tau}{t}\right)^2 \left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{1-\beta}{1+\beta} + m_{2,2} + \frac{m_1-n}{1+\beta}}\exp\left(-\left(\frac{t}{t-\tau}\right)^2\frac{\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{2^{\frac{3-\beta}{1+\beta}}C_T}\right).
    \end{align*}
    Using the exponential factor, we can eliminate the $\frac{1}{t-\tau}$ singularities in \eqref{eq:A2_v_z_int_1} and produce an arbitrary order of polynomial decay. Therefore, for any $N\geq 0$, we prove \eqref{eq:A2_v_z_int}.
    
    Next, we bound the $v_h$-integral of \eqref{eq:A2_v_z_int}: we define $C'_T$ by
    \begin{align*}
        C'_T = \frac{\beta^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}{1+\beta}2^{\frac{3}{1+\beta}}C_T,
    \end{align*}
    and compute the integral
    \begin{align*}
        \int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}|v_h|^{m_{2,1}} \left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{C'_T}\right)\,dv_h.
    \end{align*}
    
    Since $0\leq\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}\leq 1$, we crudely split the exponential function by
    \begin{align*}
        e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}&\leq e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}e^{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha|v_h(t-\tau)|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\\
        &\leq e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}e^{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}
    \end{align*}
    for $0\leq t-\tau\leq \frac{1}{4}$. Using this inequality, we get
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N} e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|\frac{t}{t-\tau}|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{C'_T}\right)\,dv_h\\
        &\leq e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N} e^{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{C'_T}\right)\,dv_h\\
        &\leq \exp\left(\frac{9C'_T\alpha^2}{4}\right)e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\\
        &\qquad \times \int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C'_T}\left(|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\right)^2\right)\,dv_h.
    \end{align*}
    To bound the final integral, we split the $v_h$ domain by
    \begin{align*}
        |v_h|(t-\tau)\leq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)
    \end{align*}
    and the other part. When $|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C'_T}\left(|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\right)^2\right)\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\leq \frac{1}{2}(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)}\,dv_h\\
        &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C'_T}\left(|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\right)^2\right)\,dv_h\\
        &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}
    \end{align*}
    as the final integral is uniformly bounded. If $|v_h|(t-\tau)\geq \frac{1}{2}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)$, we further assume
    \begin{align}\label{eq:A2_v_h_cond}
        \frac{1}{4}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)\geq \left(\frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{\beta}}.
    \end{align}
    Otherwise, since $|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1$ is bounded above,
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C'_T}\left(|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\right)^2\right)\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\geq \frac{1}{2}(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)}\,dv_h\\
        &\leq \int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C'_T}\left(|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\right)^2\right)\,dv_h,
    \end{align*}
    and the final integral is well-bounded by a constant. Under this region \eqref{eq:A2_v_h_cond}, we have
    \begin{align*}
        |v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\geq |v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \left(\frac{|v_h|(t-\tau)}{2}\right)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\geq \frac{1}{2}|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align*}
    as $0\leq t-\tau\leq \frac{1}{4}$. Thus, using Lemma~\ref{lem:exp_integral}, we get
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C'_T}\left(|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} - \frac{3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} C'_T\alpha}{2}\right)^2\right)\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\geq \frac{1}{2}(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)}\,dv_h\\
        &\leq \int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} |v_h|^{m_{2,1}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}{4C'_T}\right)\mathbf{1}_{|v_h|(t-\tau)\geq \frac{1}{2}(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)}\,dv_h\\
        &\leq C\left(\frac{1}{2(t-\tau)}\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)\right)^{n-1-\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta} + m_{2,1}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4C'_T}\left(\frac{1}{2(t-\tau)}(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)\right)^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}\right).
    \end{align*}
    Again, we can construct an arbitrary polynomial decay from the exponentially decaying term and bound the final result by $C_N\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}$ for any $N\geq 0$. It proves \eqref{eq:A2_v_h_int}. Applying the two results \eqref{eq:A2_v_z_int} and \eqref{eq:A2_v_h_int} for $C_T = 4C_5$ to \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_form1}, for any $N\geq 0$, we obtain
    \begin{align}\label{eq:M[f]_integral_res2}
        \begin{split}
            &\frac{t}{t-\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(|v_h|^{m_2} + \left(\frac{\tau}{t-\tau}|v_z|\right)^{m_2}\right) \frac{e^{-\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{n-m_1}{1+\beta}}}\\
            &\qquad \times \exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + |\frac{t}{t-\tau}v_z|^2}{4C_5(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z}{t} - v_z + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{v_z\leq -2\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z}{t}+1\right)}\,dv\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-N}e^{-3^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    Combining \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_form2} and \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_res2}, we obtain the result \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_res}.
<!-- end proof-8 -->
<!-- corollary-1: body_md -->
\label{prop:bound}
    For $1\leq \beta\leq 2$, assume
    \begin{align*}
        &\left|u-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)\leq C_3 \left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}\\
        &C_4\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}\leq T(t,x)\leq C_5\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align*}
    for some constants $C_i$ and $0<t\leq \frac{1}{4}$. Then, the following holds.
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item There exists a constant $C>0$ for each $i=0, 1$ depending on $\beta$, $n$, and $C_i$ such that
        \begin{align*}
            &\sup_{0<\tau\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \bigl|v - \tfrac{z}{t}\hat z\bigr|^{2i} \frac{1}{T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))^{\frac{n}{2}+i}} \exp\!\left(-\frac{\bigl|v - u(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))\bigr|^2}{4T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\, dv \leq C.
        \end{align*}
        \item There exists a constant $C>0$ depending on $\alpha$, $\beta$, $n$, and $C_i$ such that
        \begin{align*}
            &\sup_{0<\tau\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(|v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{t-\tau}{\tau}\left|v_z - \frac{z}{t}\right|\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}} \frac{1}{T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))^{n/2 + 1}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{4T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-\frac{2\beta}{(1+\beta)^2}}.
        \end{align*}
    \end{enumerate}
<!-- end corollary-1 -->
<!-- proof-9: body_md -->
In \eqref{eq:macro_bound}, we choose $\rho = 1$, so $\alpha = 0$, $m_1 = 0$, and $C_1 = C_2 = 1$. Choosing $m_2 = 0$, we get the result for $i=0$. If $i=1$, we choose $\rho = \left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{-\frac{2}{1+\beta}}$ and $m_2 = 2$ to get a similar result.

    For the second one, we choose $\rho = \left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1\right)^{-\frac{2}{1+\beta}}$. In \eqref{eq:M[f]_integral_form1}, we select
    \begin{align*}
        (m_{2,1}, m_{2,2}) = \left(\frac{2}{1+\beta}, 0\right)\quad\text{or}\quad(m_{2,1}, m_{2,2}) = \left(0, \frac{2}{1+\beta}\right).
    \end{align*}
    Then,
    \begin{align*}
        &\sup_{0<\tau\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(|v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{t-\tau}{\tau}\left|v_z - \frac{z}{t}\right|\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}} \frac{1}{T^{n/2 + 1}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u|^2}{4T}\right)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
        &\leq C\sup_{0<\tau\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(\left(|v_h(t-\tau)|\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}} + \left(\frac{t-\tau}{\tau}\left|v_z - \frac{z}{t}\right|\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}\right) \frac{1}{T^{n/2 + 1}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u|^2}{4T}\right)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
        &\leq C\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{-\frac{2\beta}{(1+\beta)^2}}
    \end{align*}
    for a constant $C$.
<!-- end proof-9 -->
<!-- proposition-3: body_md -->
\label{prop:apriori}
    Let $\rho_g$, $u_g$, and $T_g$ be given by \eqref{eq:macro_0_bound}. Then there exists $0<t_0\leq \frac{1}{4}$ depending on the $C_i$, $m$, $\alpha$, $\beta$, and $n$ such that the following holds. Assume $f_1$ satisfies
    \begin{align}\label{eq:macro_bound_f_1}
        \begin{split}
            &|\rho_1-\rho_g|(t,x)\leq \frac{1}{2}\rho_g(t,x),\\
            &\left|u_1-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)\leq \frac{C_3 + 1}{\sqrt{C_4}}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)},\\
            &|T_1-T_g|(t,x)\leq \frac{1}{2}T_g(t,x)
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    for $0<t\leq t_0$, where $(\rho_1,u_1,T_1)$ are macroscopic quantities generated by $f_1$. If $f_2$ satisfies
    \begin{align*}
        f_2(t,x,v) = e^{-t_b}g(t-t_b, x - vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{t\geq t_b} + \int_0^t e^{-(t - \tau)} \mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x - v(t - \tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,d\tau,
    \end{align*}
    then the $(\rho_2, u_2, T_2)$ generated by $f_2$ also satisfy \eqref{eq:macro_bound_f_1} for $0<t\leq t_0$.
<!-- end proposition-3 -->
<!-- proof-10: body_md -->
We first write $(\rho_2, u_2, T_2)$ as
    \begin{align}\label{eq:macro_f_2}
        \begin{split}
            &\rho_2(t,x)\left(1, u_2(t,x)-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}, n T_2(t,x)\right)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(1,v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z},|v-u_2(t,x)|^2\right)f_2(t,x,v)\,dv\\
            &=\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(1,v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z},|v-u_2(t,x)|^2\right)e^{-\frac{z}{v_z}}g(t-t_b, x-vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{v_z\geq \frac{z}{t}}\,dv\\
            &\quad + \int_0^t \int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(1,v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z},|v-u_2(t,x)|^2\right)e^{-(t-\tau)}\mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    The first term represents the macroscopic fields generated by the damped free transport of the boundary data $g(t,x_h,v)$. For the second term, we can bound the integral using Proposition~\ref{prop:boundary_int} and~\ref{prop:M[f]_integral} as
    \begin{align}\label{eq:macro_M(f_1)}
        \begin{split}
            &\int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(1,\left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|,\left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2\right)\mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau\\
            &\leq C_{\mathcal{M}}t\left(\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{n-\beta-m}{1+\beta}}, \left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{n+1-\beta-m}{1+\beta}}, \left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{n+2-\beta-m}{1+\beta}}\right) e^{-\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\\
            &\leq C_{\mathcal{M}}C_1^{-1}t\rho_g\left(1, \sqrt{C_4}^{-1}\sqrt{T_g}, C_4^{-1}T_g\right)(t,x)
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    for some constant $C_{\mathcal{M}}$ depending on $C_i$ in \eqref{eq:macro_0_bound} and other fixed constants in Proposition \ref{prop:M[f]_integral}. 
    From \eqref{eq:macro_0_bound}, we further have
    \begin{align*}
        \left|u_g(t,x)-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\leq \frac{C_3}{\sqrt{C_4}}\sqrt{T_g}(t,x),
    \end{align*}
    
    Now, we check each line of \eqref{eq:macro_bound_f_1}. From the definition of $\rho_g$ in Definition \ref{Def:macro_0}, we obtain
    \begin{align}\label{eq:rho_diff}
        \begin{split}
            0\leq (\rho_2-\rho_g)(t,x) &= \int_0^t  e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau\\
            &\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}t\rho_g(t,x).
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    Therefore, we choose $t\leq \frac{C_1}{2C_{\mathcal{M}}}$ to get $|\rho_2-\rho_g|(t,x)\leq \frac{1}{2}\rho_g(t,x)$. From this result, we also have $\frac{\rho_g}{\rho_2}\leq 1$.
    
    To compute $u_2(t,x)$, we directly use \eqref{eq:macro_f_2} and \eqref{eq:macro_M(f_1)} by
    \begin{align*}
        \rho_2\left|u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)&\leq \left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)e^{-\frac{z}{v_z}}g(t-t_b, x-vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{v_z\geq \frac{z}{t}}\,dv\right|\\
        &\quad + \int_0^t  e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau \\
        &\leq \rho_g\left|u_g-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x) + \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1\sqrt{C_4}}t\rho_g\sqrt{T_g}(t,x)\\
        &\leq \frac{1}{\sqrt{C_4}}\left(C_3+\frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}t\right)\rho_g\sqrt{T_g}(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    As $\frac{\rho_g}{\rho_2}\leq 1$, it shows
    \begin{align}\label{eq:u2_bound}
        \left|u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)\leq \frac{C_3 + 1}{\sqrt{C_4}}\sqrt{T_g}(t,x)
    \end{align}
    for $t\leq \frac{C_1}{C_{\mathcal{M}}}$.
    
    We can further compute $|u_2-u_g|$ as follows. From the definition of $\rho_g u_g$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \left|\rho_2\left(u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right) - \rho_g\left(u_g-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)\right|(t,x)&\leq \int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau \\
        &\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1\sqrt{C_4}}t\rho_g\sqrt{T_g}(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    From \eqref{eq:rho_diff} and \eqref{eq:u2_bound}, we get
    \begin{align*}
        \rho_g\left|u_2-u_g\right|(t,x)&\leq\left|\rho_2\left(u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right) - \rho_g\left(u_g-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)\right|(t,x) + \left|(\rho_g-\rho_2)\left(u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)\right|(t,x)\\
        &\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1\sqrt{C_4}}t\rho_g\sqrt{T_g}(t,x) + \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}\frac{C_3 + 1}{\sqrt{C_4}}t\rho_g \sqrt{T_g}(t,x)\\
        &\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}\frac{C_3 + 2}{\sqrt{C_4}}t\rho_g\sqrt{T_g}(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    It shows
    \begin{align}\label{eq:u_diff}
        \left|u_2-u_g\right|(t,x)\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}\frac{C_3 + 2}{\sqrt{C_4}}t\sqrt{T_g}(t,x).
    \end{align}
    
    Next, we compute $n|\rho_g T_g - \rho_2 T_2|$. We explicitly write down the formula and bound it by
    \begin{align}\label{eq:T_diff_1}
        \begin{split}
            n|\rho_2 T_2 - \rho_g T_g|(t,x) &\leq \left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(|v-u_2(t,x)|^2 - |v-u_g(t,x)|^2\right)e^{-\frac{z}{v_z}}g(t-t_b, x-vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{v_z\geq \frac{z}{t}}\,dv\right|\\
            &\quad + \int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left||v-u_2(t,x)|^2 - \left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2\right|\mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau\\
            &\quad + \int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2 \mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    The difference term in the first integral is given by
    \begin{align*}
        (v-u_2(t,x))^2 - (v-u_g(t,x))^2 &= \left(u_2(t,x) + u_g(t,x) - 2v\right)\cdot \left(u_2(t,x) - u_g(t,x)\right)
    \end{align*}
    By the same calculation, we also have
    \begin{align*}
        (v-u_2(t,x))^2 - \left(v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)^2=\left(u_2(t,x) + \frac{z}{t}\hat{z} - 2v\right)\cdot \left(u_2(t,x) - \frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)
    \end{align*}
    in the second integral.
    
    Using this equality and \eqref{eq:u_diff}, we bound the first integral by
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(|v-u_2(t,x)|^2 - |v-u_g(t,x)|^2\right)e^{-\frac{z}{v_z}}g(t-t_b, x-vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{v_z\geq \frac{z}{t}}\,dv\\
        &= (u_2-u_g)\cdot \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(u_2(t,x) + u_g(t,x) - 2v\right)e^{-\frac{z}{v_z}}g(t-t_b, x-vt_b,v)\mathbf{1}_{v_z\geq \frac{z}{t}}\,dv\\
        &= \rho_g (u_2-u_g)^2\leq \left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}\frac{C_3 + 2}{\sqrt{C_4}} t\right)^2\rho_g T_g(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    Using \eqref{eq:macro_M(f_1)} and \eqref{eq:u2_bound}, the second integral can be bounded similarly:
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left||v-u_2(t,x)|^2 - \left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2\right|\mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau\\
        &\leq \left|u_2(t,x) - \frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(\left|u_2(t,x) - \frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right| + 2\left|v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\right)\mathcal{M}(f_1)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv d\tau\\
        &\leq C_{\mathcal{M}}C_1^{-1} t\rho_g \left(\left|u_2 - \frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2 + 2\left|u_2 - \frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\sqrt{C_4}^{-1} \sqrt{T_g}\right)(t,x)\\
        &\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1 C_4}(C_3 + 1)(C_3+3) t \rho_g T_g(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    Finally, the last integral in \eqref{eq:T_diff_1} is bounded by $\frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1C_4}t\rho_gT_g(t,x)$ by \eqref{eq:macro_M(f_1)}. 
    
    Combining all the results, we conclude
    \begin{align*}
        n|\rho_2 T_2-\rho_g T_g|(t,x)\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1C_4}\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1} (C_3 + 2)^2 t +(C_3+1)(C_3+3) + 1\right) t\rho_g T_g(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    From this and \eqref{eq:rho_diff}, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \rho_2|T_2-T_g|&\leq |\rho_2 T_2-\rho_g T_g| + |(\rho_g - \rho_2)T_g|\\
        &\leq \frac{1}{n}\frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1C_4}\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1} (C_3 + 2)^2 t +(C_3+1)(C_3+3) + 1\right) t\rho_g T_g(t,x) + \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}t\rho_gT_g(t,x),
    \end{align*}
    and as $\frac{\rho_g}{\rho_2}\leq 1$, we get
    \begin{align*}
        |T_2-T_g|\leq \frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1}\left(\frac{1}{nC_4}\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{M}}}{C_1} (C_3 + 2)^2 t +(C_3+1)(C_3+3) + 1\right) + 1\right) tT_g(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    Now, we can choose small enough $t$ depending on $C_{\mathcal{M}}$ and $C_i$ to make
    \begin{align*}
        |T_2-T_g|(t,x)\leq \frac{1}{2}T_g(t,x).
    \end{align*}
<!-- end proof-10 -->
<!-- proposition-4: body_md -->
\label{prop:maxwell}
    For given inflow boundary data $g$ in \eqref{eq:boundary_data}, assume that $f_1,f_2\in \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0}$. By \eqref{eq:X_T_A3}, there exist constants $C_{u,1}$ and $C_{u,2}$ such that
    \begin{align*}
        \left|u_1-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)&\leq C_{u,1}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)}\\
        \left|u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)&\leq C_{u,2}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)},
    \end{align*}
    where $(\rho_i, u_i, T_i)$ are the macroscopic fields generated by $f_i$ for $i=1,2$. Then 
    \begin{align}\label{Inequ:maxwell}
        \begin{split}
            &\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\left|\mathcal{M}(f_1)-\mathcal{M}(f_2)\right|(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
            &\leq C\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\mathfrak{w}(\tau,x,v)|f_1-f_2|(\tau,x,v)\,dv
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    for almost every $0<\tau\leq t$ and all $0<t\leq t_0$. Here, the constant $C<\infty$ depends on the boundary data $g$ in \eqref{eq:boundary_data}, $\max\{C_{u,1}, C_{u,2}\}$, and dimension $n$.
<!-- end proposition-4 -->
<!-- proof-11: body_md -->
First, we define a linear combination of $f_1$ and $f_2$ by $f_\lambda = (1-\lambda)f_1 + \lambda f_2$ for $0\leq \lambda\leq 1$. The corresponding macroscopic fields are defined as follows:
    \begin{align*}
        \rho_\lambda &= \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}(1-\lambda)f_1 + \lambda f_2\,dv,\\
        \rho_\lambda u_\lambda &= \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}v\left((1-\lambda)f_1 + \lambda f_2\right)\,dv,\\
        \rho_\lambda\left(nT_\lambda + u_\lambda^2\right) &= \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}v^2\left((1-\lambda)f_1 + \lambda f_2\right)\,dv.
    \end{align*}
    We first claim that $\rho_\lambda$, $u_\lambda$, and $T_\lambda$ satisfy
    \begin{align}\label{eq:macro_lambda_bound}
        \begin{split}
            &|\rho_\lambda-\rho_g|(t,x)\leq \frac{1}{2}\rho_g(t,x),\\
            &\left|u_\lambda-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)\leq C_{u,max}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)},\\
            &\frac{1}{2}T_g(t,x)\leq T_\lambda(t,x)\leq \left(C_{u,max}^2 + \frac{3}{2}\right)T_g(t,x)
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    for all $0\leq \lambda\leq 1$, where $C_{u,max} =\max\{C_{u,1},C_{u,2}\}$, and $C_3$ and $C_4$ are from \eqref{eq:macro_0_bound}. Indeed, $\rho_\lambda$ is a linear combination of $\rho_1$ and $\rho_2$, so it satisfies
    \begin{align*}
        |\rho_\lambda-\rho_g|(t,x)\leq \frac{1}{2}\rho_g(t,x)
    \end{align*}
    by \eqref{eq:X_T_A3}.
    
    For $u_\lambda$, we write
    \begin{align*}
        \left(u_\lambda - \frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)(t,x) = \frac{1}{\rho_\lambda}\left((1-\lambda)\rho_1\left(u_1-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right) + \lambda\rho_2\left(u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)\right),
    \end{align*}
    so we have
    \begin{align*}
        \left|u_\lambda-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|(t,x)\leq \max\left\{\left|u_1-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|,\left|u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\right\}\leq \max\{C_{u,1},C_{u,2}\}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)}.
    \end{align*}
    
    For $T_\lambda$, it is given by
    \begin{align*}
        n T_\lambda = \frac{1}{\rho_\lambda}\left((1-\lambda)\rho_1(n T_1 + u_1^2) + \lambda\rho_2(n T_2 + u_2^2) - \rho_\lambda u_\lambda^2\right).
    \end{align*}
    By an explicit calculation, we have
    \begin{align*}
        (1-\lambda) \rho_1 u_1^2 +\lambda\rho_2u_2^2 - \rho_\lambda u_\lambda^2 &=(1-\lambda) \rho_1 u_1^2 +\lambda\rho_2u_2^2 - \frac{((1-\lambda)\rho_1u_1 + \lambda\rho_2u_2)^2}{(1-\lambda) \rho_1 + \lambda\rho_2}\\
        &=\lambda(1-\lambda)\rho_1\rho_2\frac{u_1^2 + u_2^2 - 2u_1\cdot u_2}{(1-\lambda) \rho_1 + \lambda\rho_2}\\
        &=\lambda(1-\lambda)\rho_1\rho_2\frac{(u_1-u_2)^2}{\rho_\lambda}.
    \end{align*}
    Therefore,
    \begin{align*}
        nT_\lambda = \frac{\lambda(1-\lambda)\rho_1\rho_2}{\rho_\lambda^2}|u_1-u_2|^2 + \frac{(1-\lambda) \rho_1}{\rho_\lambda} nT_1 + \frac{\lambda \rho_2}{\rho_\lambda} nT_2.
    \end{align*}
    The upper and lower bounds of $T_\lambda - T_g$ are computed as follows.
    \begin{align*}
        n(T_\lambda - T_g) &= \frac{\lambda(1-\lambda)\rho_1\rho_2}{\rho_\lambda^2}|u_1-u_2|^2 + \frac{(1-\lambda) \rho_1}{\rho_\lambda} nT_1 + \frac{\lambda \rho_2}{\rho_\lambda} nT_2 - nT_g\\
        &\leq \frac{1}{4}|u_1-u_2|^2 + \frac{n}{2}T_g,\\
        n(T_\lambda - T_g) &\geq \frac{(1-\lambda) \rho_1}{\rho_\lambda} nT_1 + \frac{\lambda \rho_2}{\rho_\lambda} nT_2 - nT_g\geq -n\max\{|T_1-T_g|,|T_2-T_g|\}\\
        &\geq -\frac{n}{2}T_g.
    \end{align*}
    Since
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{\lambda(1-\lambda)\rho_1\rho_2}{((1-\lambda)\rho_1 + \lambda \rho_2)^2}\leq \frac{1}{4}\quad \text{and}\quad |u_1-u_2|\leq \left|u_1-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right| + \left|u_2-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|\leq 2\max\{C_{u,1},C_{u,2}\}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)},
    \end{align*}
    we get
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{1}{2}T_g(t,x)\leq T_\lambda(t,x)\leq \left((\max\{C_{u,1},C_{u,2}\})^2 + \frac{3}{2}\right)T_g(t,x).
    \end{align*}
    This proves the claim.

    By the Fundamental Theorem of Calculus, the left-hand side of \eqref{Inequ:maxwell} can be written as
    \begin{align}\label{eq:contract_prep1}
        \begin{split}
            &\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\left|\mathcal{M}(f_1)-\mathcal{M}(f_2)\right|(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv\\
            &\leq \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\int_0^1 \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\left|\dv{\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,d\lambda dv.
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    Since the local Maxwellian depends smoothly on the macroscopic fields
    $(\rho_\lambda,u_\lambda,T_\lambda)$, differentiation with respect to the
    interpolation parameter $\lambda$ yields
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{d}{d\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda):=\frac{d\rho_\lambda}{d\lambda}\frac{\partial}{\partial\rho_\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)+\frac{d u_\lambda}{d\lambda}\frac{\partial}{\partial u_\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)+\frac{d T_\lambda}{d\lambda}\frac{\partial}{\partial T_\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda).
    \end{align*}
    Now, the following equalities can be obtained by a straightforward differentiation starting from the definitions of $\rho_\lambda$, $u_\lambda$, and $T_\lambda$.
    \begin{align*}
        \dv{\lambda}\rho_\lambda(t,x) &= \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}(f_2-f_1)(t,x,w)\,dw,\\
        \frac{\rho_\lambda^2}{\rho_1}\dv{\lambda} u_\lambda(t,x) &=
        \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)(f_2-f_1)(t,x,w)\,dw - \left(u_1(t,x)-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}(f_2-f_1)(t,x,w)\,dw,\\
        n\frac{\rho_\lambda^2}{\rho_1}\dv{\lambda} T_\lambda(t,x) &=\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)^2(f_2-f_1)(t,x,w)\,dw - \left(nT_1 + \left(u_1 - \frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)^2\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}(f_2-f_1)(t,x,w)\,dw\\
        &\quad - 2\left(u_\lambda-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right) \cdot\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)(f_2-f_1)(t,x,w)\,dw - \left(u_1-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}(f_2-f_1)(t,x,w)\,dw\right).
    \end{align*}
    Generally, the same conclusion remains valid when $\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}$ is replaced by any fixed vector $c(t,x)$. From \eqref{eq:X_T_A3} and \eqref{eq:macro_lambda_bound}, we further simplify these equations by
    \begin{align}\label{eq:macro_deri}
        \begin{split}
            \left|\dv{\lambda}\rho_\lambda\right|(t,x) &\leq  \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw,\\
            \frac{\rho_\lambda^2}{\rho_1}\left|\dv{\lambda} u_\lambda\right|(t,x) &\leq
            \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right||f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw + C_{u,max}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw,\\
            n\frac{\rho_\lambda^2}{\rho_1}\left|\dv{\lambda} T_\lambda\right|(t,x) &\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw + C T_g(t,x)\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw\\
            &\quad + 2C_{u,max}\sqrt{T_g(t,x)} \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right||f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw,
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    where $C = \frac{3n}{2} +3C_{u,max}^2$.
    
    We next estimate the derivative of the local Maxwellian $\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)$. Combining \eqref{eq:macro_deri} with the explicit form of the local Maxwellian, we obtain
    \begin{align*}
        \left|\dv{\rho_\lambda}{\lambda}\dv{\rho_\lambda} \mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|(t,x,v)&\leq \frac{1}{(2\pi T_\lambda)^{n/2}} \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{2T_\lambda}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw,\\
        \left|\dv{u_\lambda}{\lambda}\dv{u_\lambda} \mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|(t,x,v)&\leq \frac{\rho_1\rho_\lambda^{-1}}{(2\pi T_\lambda)^{n/2}} \frac{|v - u_\lambda|}{T_\lambda} \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{2T_\lambda}\right)\\
        &\qquad \times \left( \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right||f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw + C_{u,max}\sqrt{T_g}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw\right),\\
        \left|\dv{T_\lambda}{\lambda}\dv{T_\lambda} \mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|(t,x,v)&\leq \frac{\rho_1\rho_\lambda^{-1}}{n(2\pi T_\lambda)^{n/2}}\left(\frac{n}{2}\frac{1}{T_\lambda} + \frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{2T_\lambda^2}\right) \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{2T_\lambda}\right)\\
        &\qquad \times \left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw + CT_g\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw\right.\\
        &\qquad \quad \left.+ 2C_{u,max}\sqrt{T_g} \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right||f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw\right).
    \end{align*}
    Now, we use the elementary inequalities 
    \[
    x e^{-\frac{x^2}{4}} \le 1, \qquad
    x^2 e^{-\frac{x^2}{4}} \le 2 \quad \text{for } x \ge 0.
    \]
    for \( x = \frac{|v-u_\lambda|}{\sqrt{T_\lambda}} \) to get
    \begin{align*}
        |u_\lambda - v| \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{4T_\lambda}\right)\leq \sqrt{T_\lambda},\quad |u_\lambda - v|^2\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{4T_\lambda}\right)\leq 2T_\lambda.
    \end{align*}
    Substituting these bounds into the previous estimates with some rearrangements, we obtain
    \begin{align*}
        \left|\dv{\rho_\lambda}{\lambda}\dv{\rho_\lambda} \mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)(t,x,v)\right|&\leq \frac{2\pi}{(2\pi T_\lambda)^{n/2 + 1}} \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{4T_\lambda}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}T_\lambda |f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw,\\
        \left|\dv{u_\lambda}{\lambda}\dv{u_\lambda} \mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)(t,x,v)\right|&\leq \frac{2\pi\rho_1\rho_\lambda^{-1}}{(2\pi T_\lambda)^{n/2 + 1}} \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{4T_\lambda}\right)\\
        &\qquad \times \left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\sqrt{T_\lambda}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right||f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw + C_{u,max}\sqrt{T_\lambda T_g}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw\right),\\
        \left|\dv{T_\lambda}{\lambda}\dv{T_\lambda} \mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)(t,x,v)\right|&\leq \frac{2\pi\rho_1\rho_\lambda^{-1}}{(2\pi T_\lambda)^{n/2 + 1}}\left(\frac{1}{2} + \frac{1}{n}\right) \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{4T_\lambda}\right)\\
        &\qquad \times \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right|^2 + 2C_{u,max}\sqrt{T_g}\left|w-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right| +  CT_g\right)|f_2-f_1|(t,x,w)\,dw.
    \end{align*}
    From \eqref{eq:macro_lambda_bound}, $\rho_1 \rho_\lambda^{-1}\leq 3$ and $T_\lambda \leq \left(C_{u,max}^2 + \frac{3}{2}\right) T_g$, so combining all the bounds, we get
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\left|\dv{\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv\\
        &\leq \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\left(\left|\frac{d\rho_\lambda}{d\lambda}\frac{\partial}{\partial\rho_\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|+\left|\frac{d u_\lambda}{d\lambda}\frac{\partial}{\partial u_\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|+\left|\frac{d T_\lambda}{d\lambda}\frac{\partial}{\partial T_\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|\right)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv\\
        &\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\frac{\mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)}{(2\pi T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau)))^{n/2 + 1}} \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{4T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\\
        &\qquad \times \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(\left|w-\frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau}\hat{z}\right| + \sqrt{T_g(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)^2 |f_2-f_1|(\tau, x-v(t-\tau),w)\,dw \,dv\\
        &\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\frac{\mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)}{(2\pi T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau)))^{n/2 + 1}} \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{4T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0} \,dv\\
        &\qquad \times \operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(\left|w-\frac{z}{\tau}\hat{z}\right| + \sqrt{T_g(\tau, x)}\right)^2 |f_2-f_1|(\tau, x,w)\,dw.
    \end{align*}
    for a constant $C$ depending on the dimension $n$ and $C_u$ for almost every $x$ and $\tau$.
    
    As $T_g(\tau,x)\leq C_5\left(|x_h|+\frac{z}{\tau} + 1\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}$ from \eqref{eq:macro_0_bound}, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\left(\left|w-\frac{z}{\tau}\hat{z}\right| + \sqrt{T_g(\tau, x)}\right)^2 |f_2-f_1|(\tau, x,w)\,dw\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\mathfrak{w}(\tau,x,w)|f_2-f_1|(\tau,x,w)\,dw.
    \end{align*}
    Summarizing the current result, we have
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\left|\dv{\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv\\
        &\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\frac{1}{(2\pi T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau)))^{n/2 + 1}} \exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{4T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv\\
        &\qquad \times\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\mathfrak{w}(\tau,x,w)|f_2-f_1|(\tau,x,w)\,dw.
    \end{align*}
    Our remaining task is to bound the $v$-integral. We first decompose the $x_h$ and $\frac{z}{t}$ in $\mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)$ by
    \begin{align*}
        x_h = x_h-v_h(t-\tau) + v_h(t-\tau)\quad \text{and} \quad \frac{z}{t} = \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + \frac{(t-\tau)}{\tau}\left(v_z-\frac{z}{t}\right)
    \end{align*}
    to extract $x_h-v_h(t-\tau) + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau}$ of $T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))$. Separating the terms, we rearrange the integral by
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(1+(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t})^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}} + \left(v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)^2\right) \frac{1}{T_\lambda^{n/2 + 1}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda|^2}{4T_\lambda}\right)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
        &\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(1+\left(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}} + \left(|v_h(t-\tau)|+\left|\frac{(t-\tau)}{\tau}\left(v_z-\frac{z}{t}\right)\right|\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}} + \left(v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)^2\right)\\
        &\qquad \times \frac{1}{T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))^{n/2 + 1}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{4T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
        &\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \frac{1}{T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))^{n/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{4T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
        &\quad + \int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \left(\left(|v_h(t-\tau)|+\frac{t-\tau}{\tau}\left|(v_z - \frac{z}{t})\right|\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}} + \left(v-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}\right)^2\right)\\
        &\qquad \times \frac{1}{T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))^{n/2 + 1}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{4T_\lambda(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv.
    \end{align*}
    Applying Proposition~\ref{prop:boundary_int} to \eqref{eq:macro_lambda_bound}, we obtain an upper bound of order
    $(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1)^{\frac{1}{1+\beta}}$ for $|u_\lambda-\frac{z}{t}\hat{z}|$ and two-sided bounds of order $(|x_h|+\frac{z}{t}+1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}$ for $T_\lambda$. Thus, we can directly employ Corollary~\ref{prop:bound} to bound the two integrals by a uniform constant. In conclusion, we obtain the following result
    \begin{align*}
        &\sup_{0\leq \lambda\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\left|\dv{\lambda}\mathcal{M}(f_\lambda)\right|(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,dv\\
        &\leq C\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n}\mathfrak{w}(\tau,x,w)|f_2-f_1|(\tau,x,w)\,dw
    \end{align*}
    for almost every $0<\tau\leq t$ and $x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+$ for all $0<t\leq t_0$. Since the constant $C$ only depends on the inflow boundary data $g$, $C_{u,max}$, and the dimension $n$, by \eqref{eq:contract_prep1}, we prove the proposition.
<!-- end proof-11 -->
<!-- theorem-3: body_md -->
\label{thm:BGK_sol}
        For the inflow boundary data $g$ defined in \eqref{eq:boundary_data} and the initial data $f_0(x,v)=0$ for $x\in \mathbb{R}^n_+$, there exists $t_0>0$ and a unique solution $f(t,x,v)$ of the BGK model in the space $\Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0}$. Furthermore, we can choose a version of $f(t,x,v)$ such that it satisfies \eqref{eq:X_T_A3} pointwise.
<!-- end theorem-3 -->
<!-- proof-12: body_md -->
For given inflow boundary data $g$ in \eqref{eq:boundary_data}, we define the solution map $\mathcal{T}$ by
    \begin{align*}
        \mathcal{T}f(t,x,v) := e^{-t_b}g(t-t_b, x - vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{t\geq t_b} + \int_0^t e^{-(t - \tau)} \mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x - v(t - \tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,d\tau.
    \end{align*}
    From Proposition~\ref{prop:boundary_int}, we have the macroscopic fields bounds \eqref{eq:macro_0_bound} of $(\rho_g,u_g,T_g)$ generated by $e^{-t_b}g(t-t_b, x - vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{t\geq t_b}$. We choose $C_u = \frac{C_3+1}{\sqrt{C_4}}$ and $0<t_0^*\leq \frac{1}{4}$ depending on the inflow boundary data $g$ from Proposition~\ref{prop:apriori}. We construct a function sequence by
    \begin{align*}
        f^1(t,x,v) = e^{-t_b}g(t-t_b, x - vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{t\geq t_b},
    \end{align*}
    and $f^i = \mathcal{T}f^{i-1}$ for $i\geq 2$. Then, we claim that $\{f^i\}_{i\in\mathbb{N}}\subset \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0^*}$. Indeed,
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item by the construction, $f^i(0,x,v) = 0$ for all $i\geq 1$.
        \item By Proposition~\ref{prop:apriori}, since $f^1$ trivially satisfies \eqref{eq:macro_bound_f_1} by definition, $f^i = \mathcal{T}f^{i-1}$ for all $i\geq 2$ satisfies \eqref{eq:macro_bound_f_1}, so \eqref{eq:X_T_A3} for all $(t,x)$.
        \item Since all $f^i$ satisfies \eqref{eq:X_T_A3} in the pointwise sense, using Propositions~\ref{prop:boundary_int} and \ref{prop:M[f]_integral}, we have
        \begin{align*}
            &\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)e^{-t_b}g(t-t_b, x - vt_b, v)\mathbf{1}_{t\geq t_b}\,dv\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{n+2-\beta-m}{1+\beta}}e^{-\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}},
            \intertext{and}
            &\sup_{0<\tau\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)\mathcal{M}(f^i)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv\\
            &\leq C\left(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t} + 1\right)^{\frac{n+2-\beta-m}{1+\beta}}e^{-\alpha(|x_h| + \frac{z}{t}+1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}
        \end{align*}
        for all $0<t\leq t_0^*$ and $x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+$. Therefore,
        \begin{align*}
            \sup_{0<t\leq t_0^*}\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)f^i(t,x,v)\,dv\leq C<\infty
        \end{align*}
        for a constant $C$ independent of $i$.
    \end{enumerate}
    Now, we can employ Proposition~\ref{prop:maxwell} for $f^i$: for $f^i$ and $f^{i-1}$ for $i\geq 2$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        &\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|\mathcal{T}f^i - \mathcal{T}f^{i-1}|(t,x,v)\,dv\\
        &\leq\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_0^t\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|\mathcal{M}(f^i)-\mathcal{M}(f^{i-1})|(\tau,x-v(t-\tau),v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv d\tau\\
        &\leq Ct\sup_{0< \tau\leq t}\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+} \int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(\tau,x,v)|f^i-f^{i-1}|(\tau,x,v) \,dv
    \end{align*}
    for all $0<t\leq t_0^*$; since the macroscopic fields of $f^i$ satisfy \eqref{eq:X_T_A3} pointwise, we can use $\sup$ instead of $\operatorname*{ess\,sup}$. We choose $t_0 = \min\{\frac{1}{2C}, t^*_0\}$. Since $\Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta},t_0^*}\subset \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta},t_0}$, we have $\{f^i\}_{i\in\mathbb{N}}\subset \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta},t_0}$. Also, we obtain
    \begin{align*}
        \sup_{0<t\leq t_0} \sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|\mathcal{T}f^i-\mathcal{T}f^{i-1}|(t,x,v)\,dv\leq \frac{1}{2}\sup_{0<t\leq t_0} \sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|f^i - f^{i-1}|(t, x,v) \,dv,
    \end{align*}
    so it is a contraction mapping $\mathcal{T}$ for $\{f^i\}$ on $\Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0}\subset L^\infty_{t,x}((0,t_0]\times\mathbb{R}^n_+, L^1_v(\mathfrak{w}))$. By the standard measure theoretic argument, we have $f^i(t,x,v)\rightarrow f(t,x,v) = f^1 + \sum_{i=2}^\infty (f^i-f^{i-1})$ (i) in $L^\infty_{t,x}((0,t_0]\times\mathbb{R}^n_+, L^1_v(\mathfrak{w}))$ and (ii) almost everywhere in $(t,x,v)$. Furthermore, the macroscopic fields of $f$ satisfy \eqref{eq:X_T_A3} for all $(t,x)$ since
    \begin{align*}
        \sum_{i=2}^\infty \sup_{0<t\leq t_0} \sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+} \int \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|f^i-f^{i-1}|(t,x,v)\,dv<\infty
    \end{align*}
    and the density of $f^i$ are all bounded below by $\frac{1}{2}\rho_g$. Therefore, it satisfies $f\in \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0}$ and $\mathcal{T}f = f$ with $f(0,x,v) = 0$. This proves the existence part.
    
    For the uniqueness, assume there exists another solution $h\in \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta},t_0}$ with some $C_u'$ in \eqref{eq:X_T_A3}. For $t>0$, by Proposition~\ref{prop:maxwell}, we have
    \begin{align*}
        &\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|f - h|(t,x,v)\,dv\\
        &=\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|\mathcal{T}f - \mathcal{T}h|(t,x,v)\,dv\\
        &\leq \operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_0^t\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|\mathcal{M}(f)-\mathcal{M}(h)|(\tau,x-v(t-\tau),v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,dv d\tau\\
        &\leq C\int_0^t \operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+} \int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(\tau,x,v)|f-h|(\tau,x,v) \,dv\,d\tau
    \end{align*}
    for almost every $0<t\leq t_0$ and for some constant $C$ depending on the boundary data $g$ in \eqref{eq:boundary_data}, $\max\{C_{u,1}, C_{u,2}\}$, and dimension $n$. By Gronwall's inequality, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{0<t\leq t_0}\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n_+}\int_{\mathbb{R}^n} \mathfrak{w}(t,x,v)|f - h|(t,x,v)\,dv = 0.
    \end{align*}
    By the definition of $\Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0}$, $h(0,x,v) = 0$ at $t=0$. Therefore, we conclude $f=h$ a.e. in $\Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0}$.
<!-- end proof-12 -->
<!-- proposition-5: body_md -->
\label{prop:Ill_posed}
    For the given boundary data $g(t,x_h,v)$ in \eqref{eq:boundary_data}, let $f(t,x,v)\in \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t_0}$ be the solution of the BGK model constructed in Theorem~\ref{thm:BGK_sol}. Then, for any $0<\alpha'\leq \alpha$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \inf_{0<t\leq t_0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n, L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+))} = \inf_{0<t\leq t_0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+, L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n))} = \infty
    \end{align*}
    for any $1\leq p,q\leq \infty$. Furthermore, let $\tilde{f}(t,x,v)$ be any solution on $0\leq t\leq t^*_0$, where $t^*_0\geq t_0$, such that $\tilde{f}(t,x,v) = f(t,x,v)$ for $0\leq t\leq t_0$. Then, it (more precisely, a version of $\tilde{f}$) satisfies
    \begin{align*}
        \inf_{0<t\leq t^*_0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}\tilde{f}(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n, L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+))} = \inf_{0<t\leq t^*_0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}\tilde{f}(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+, L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n))} = \infty
    \end{align*}
    for any $1\leq p,q\leq \infty$. Here, we need not assume that $\tilde{f}(t,x,v)$ belongs to $\Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta}, t^*_0}$.
<!-- end proposition-5 -->
<!-- proof-13: body_md -->
We directly prove the ill-posedness on any extended time interval since it includes the first statement. For notational convenience, we present the proof for $t_0^*=\infty$ and show that
    \begin{align*}
        \inf_{t>0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n, L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+))} = \inf_{t>0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+, L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n))} = \infty
    \end{align*}
    using only the assumption that $f(t,x,v) \in \Omega_{\frac{1}{1+\beta},t_0}$. The same argument applies to any finite $t_0^*$ by restricting $t$ to $0<t\leq t_0^*$.

    Let $0<\alpha'\leq \alpha$. We first check
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lp_vLq_x_res}
        \inf_{t>0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n, L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+))} = \infty.
    \end{align}
    By the definition of the solution, we have
    \begin{align*}
        e^{\alpha' |v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\geq \int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}e^{\alpha' |v_h|^\beta}\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)> 0}\,d\tau.
    \end{align*}
    Taking $L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+)$ on both sides, we write
    \begin{align*}
        \int_{\mathbb{R}^n_+} \left(e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta} f(t,x,v)\right)^q\,dx\geq \int_{\mathbb{R}^n} e^{q\alpha' |v_h|^\beta}\left(\int_0^t e^{-(t-\tau)}\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z\geq v_z(t-\tau)}\mathbf{1}_{z\geq 0}\,d\tau\right)^q\,dx.
    \end{align*}
    We confine $v_z> 0$, so $\mathbf{1}_{z> v_z(t-\tau)}\mathbf{1}_{z> 0} = \mathbf{1}_{z> v_z(t-\tau)}$.

    Our goal is to show that for any fixed
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lp_vLq_x_choice2}
        t>0,\quad \frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2}\leq z, \tau\leq \min\{t, t_0\},\quad\text{and}\quad 0< v_z\leq \frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2t},
    \end{align}
    there exists sufficiently large $M>0$ depending on $t$, $t_0$, $\alpha$, $\alpha'$, $\beta$, and $C_T$ such that for any $|v_h|\geq M$ and $x_h$ satisfying
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lp_vLq_x_choice3}
        \left(\left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + t\right)|v_h|\leq |x_h|\leq 2\left(\left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + t\right)|v_h|,
    \end{align}
    the local Maxwellian satisfies
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lp_vLq_x_1}
        e^{\alpha' |v_h|^\beta}\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\geq e^{\frac{\alpha'}{8}|v_h|^\beta}.
    \end{align}
    If we prove this, then for $1\leq q<\infty$, we obtain
    \begin{align*}
        &\int_{\mathbb{R}^n_+} \left(e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right)^q\,dx\\
        &\geq e^{-qt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} \int_{\frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2}}^{\min\{t, t_0\}} \Bigg(\int_{\frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2}}^{\min\{t, t_0\}} e^{\alpha' |v_h|^\beta}\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\\
        &\qquad \times \mathbf{1}_{\left(\left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + t\right)|v_h|\leq |x_h|\leq 2\left(\left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + t\right)|v_h|}\mathbf{1}_{z-v_z(t-\tau)>0}\,d\tau\Bigg)^q\,dz dx_h\\
        &\geq e^{-qt}e^{\frac{q\alpha'}{8}|v_h|^\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}} \int_{\frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2}}^{\min\{t, t_0\}}\Bigg(\int_{\frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2}}^{\min\{t, t_0\}}\mathbf{1}_{\left(\left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + t\right)|v_h|\leq |x_h|\leq 2\left(\left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + t\right)|v_h|}\,d\tau\Bigg)^q\,dz dx_h\\
        &\geq C\left(\min\{t, t_0\}\right)^{q+1}\left(\left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + t\right)^{n-1} e^{-qt}e^{\frac{q\alpha'}{8}|v_h|^\beta} |v_h|^{n-1}
    \end{align*}
    for any $t>0$ and $0< v_z\leq \frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2t}$. This yields the desired ill-posedness \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_res} for any $1\leq p\leq \infty$ due to the exponential term $e^{\frac{\alpha'}{8}|v_h|^\beta}$. If $q=\infty$, it is simpler as
    \begin{align*}
        \left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^\infty_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+)} \geq e^{-t}\frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2}e^{\frac{\alpha'}{8}|v_h|^\beta}\mathbf{1}_{0< v_z\leq \frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2t}},
    \end{align*}
    and we again get the ill-posedness \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_res} for $1\leq p\leq \infty$.
    
   We first derive an explicit lower bound for the $\mathcal M(f)$-term. By the choices of $z, \tau$, and $v_z$ in \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_choice2}, we have
    \begin{align}\label{eq:z_v_z_cond}
        z - v_z(t-\tau) > 0\quad \text{and}\quad \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau}\leq 2.
    \end{align}
    We estimate $\frac{|v-u(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{2T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}$. From \eqref{eq:X_T_A3}, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \left|u(\tau, x-v(t-\tau)) - \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau}\hat{z}\right|\leq C_u\sqrt{T_g(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}.
    \end{align*}
    Therefore, from $(x-y)^2\leq 2(x^2 + y^2)$, we have
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{|v-u(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{2T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}&\leq \frac{|v|^2 + |u(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\\
        &\leq \frac{|v|^2 + \left(\left|\frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau}\right| + C_u\sqrt{T_g(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)^2}{T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\\
        &\leq C + \frac{|v|^2}{T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}
    \end{align*}
    for some constant $C$. Thus, we can write
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lp_vLq_x_2}
        \begin{split}   
            &\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\\
            &=\frac{\rho(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}{(2\pi T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau)))^{n/2}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v-u(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))|^2}{2T(\tau, x-v(t-\tau))}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}\\
            &\geq C \frac{e^{-\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1)^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z - v_z(t-\tau)>0}
        \end{split}
    \end{align}
    for some constant $C_T$, replacing $\rho$ and $T$ by \eqref{eq:X_T_A3} and \eqref{eq:macro_0_bound}.
    
    By the cutoff condition \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_choice3} and \eqref{eq:z_v_z_cond}, for $|v_h|\geq 1$, it satisfies
    \begin{align*}
        \left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}}|v_h|\leq |x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1\leq \left(\left(\frac{4}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + 4t + 4\right)|v_h|.
    \end{align*}
    Indeed,
    \begin{align*}
        &|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1\geq |x_h|-|v_h|(t-\tau)\geq \left(\frac{2}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}}|v_h|,\\
        &|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1\leq |x_h|+|v_h|(t-\tau) + 3 \leq \left(\left(\frac{4}{\alpha'C_T}\right)^{\frac{1+\beta}{2}} + 4t + 4\right)|v_h|
    \end{align*}
    for $|v_h|\geq 1$. Applying these inequalities to \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_2}, we obtain
    \begin{align*}
        e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z> v_z(t-\tau)}\geq C e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}\frac{e^{-\alpha (C')^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}} |v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{(C'|v_h|)^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\alpha'\frac{|v|^2}{|v_h|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)
    \end{align*}
    for some constant $C'$.
    
    If we take $|v_h|$ large enough so that $|v_z|^2\leq \frac{1}{2}|v_h|^2$, then
    \begin{align*}
        e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}e^{-\alpha(C')^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\alpha'\frac{|v|^2}{|v_h|^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)&\geq e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}e^{-\alpha(C')^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}|v_h|^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{3}{4}\alpha'|v_h|^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}\right).
    \end{align*}
    When $1\leq \beta\leq 2$, we have $\beta\geq \frac{\beta}{1+\beta}$ and $\beta \geq \frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}$. Therefore, the exponential growth term dominates the other decaying terms as $|v_h|\rightarrow\infty$, and there exists a sufficiently large $M>0$ such that the desired result \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_1} holds for $|v_h|\geq M$.\\

    Next, we perform the $v$-integral first and then prove the ill-posedness
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lq_xLp_v_res}
        \inf_{t>0}\left\|e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}f(t,x,v)\right\|_{L^q_x(\mathbb{R}^n_+, L^p_v(\mathbb{R}^n))} = \infty.
    \end{align}
    Similar to \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_choice2}, we fix
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lq_xLp_v_choice}
        t>0,\quad \frac{\min\{t, t_0\}}{2}\leq z, \tau\leq \min\{t, t_0\},\quad 0< v_z\leq \frac{z}{4t}.
    \end{align}
    For $|v_h|$, we take scaling
    \begin{align}\label{eq:Lq_xLp_v_v_h_cond}
        |v_h| = c(|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align}
    for $0<c\leq \frac{1}{4\max\{t, 1\}}$. Under these choices, we have
    \begin{align*}
        v_z\leq 1,\quad \frac{3}{8}\leq \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau}\leq 2,
    \end{align*}
    and
    \begin{align*}
        \left|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)\right| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1&\geq |x_h| + 1 - \frac{1}{4}\left(|x_h| + 1\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}\geq \frac{3}{4}\left(|x_h| + 1\right),\\
        \left|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)\right| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1&\leq|x_h|+ 3 + \frac{1}{4}\left(|x_h| + 1\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}\\
        &\leq 4\left(|x_h|+ 1\right)
    \end{align*}
    for $1\leq \beta\leq 2$.
    
    Applying these inequalities to \eqref{eq:Lp_vLq_x_2}, we have
    \begin{align*}
        &e^{\alpha' |v_h|^\beta}\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z> v_z(t-\tau)}\\
        &\geq C e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}\frac{e^{-\alpha(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1)^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v|^2}{C_T(|x_h-v_h(t-\tau)| + \frac{z-v_z(t-\tau)}{\tau} + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{z> v_z(t-\tau)}\\
        &\geq C e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}\frac{e^{-4^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha (|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{4^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}(|x_h|+ 1)^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + 1}{\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T(|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right).
    \end{align*}
    Finally, directly replacing $|v_h|$ by \eqref{eq:Lq_xLp_v_v_h_cond}, we get
    \begin{align*}
        &e^{\alpha'|v_h|^\beta}\frac{e^{-4^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha (|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{4^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}(|x_h|+ 1)^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{|v_h|^2 + 1}{\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T(|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right)\\
        &\geq e^{\alpha'c^\beta (|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}\frac{e^{-4^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}\alpha (|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{\beta}{1+\beta}}}}{4^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}(|x_h|+ 1)^{\frac{m+\beta}{1+\beta}}}\exp\left(-\frac{c^2(|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{4}{1+\beta}} + 1}{\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}C_T(|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}}\right).
    \end{align*}
    
    If $1<\beta\leq 2$, as $\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}> \frac{\beta}{1+\beta}, \frac{2}{1+\beta}$, the first growth term overwhelms the other decay terms as $|x_h|\rightarrow \infty$ for $c>0$. Therefore, there exists sufficiently large $M>0$ and small $\epsilon>0$ such that
    \begin{align*}
        e^{\alpha' |v_h|^\beta}\mathcal{M}(f)(\tau, x-v(t-\tau), v)\mathbf{1}_{z\geq v_z(t-\tau)} \geq e^{\epsilon (|x_h| + 1)^{\frac{2\beta}{1+\beta}}}
    \end{align*}
    for $|x_h|\geq M$ under the choices of \eqref{eq:Lq_xLp_v_choice} and
    \begin{align*}
        \frac{1}{8\max\{t, 1\}}(|x_h|+1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}\leq |v_h|\leq \frac{1}{4\max\{t, 1\}}(|x_h|+1)^{\frac{2}{1+\beta}}
    \end{align*}
    from \eqref{eq:Lq_xLp_v_v_h_cond}.
    
    If $\beta = 1$, then the leading term in the exponents is given by
    \begin{align*}
        \left(\alpha'c -\frac{4}{3}\frac{c^2}{C_T}\right)(|x_h| + 1).
